<section aria-label="Promo Partner" class="relative bg-gradient-to-b from-gray-50/80 to-white dark:from-slate-700/40 dark:to-surface-dark py-8 md:py-10">\r\n
    <div class="relative max-w-[1170px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6" x-data="{\r\n
        slide: 0,\r\n
        timer: null,\r\n
        totalSlides: 1,\r\n
        paused: false,\r\n
        windowWidth: window.innerWidth,\r\n
        reducedMotion: window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches,\r\n
        init() {\r\n
            if (!this.reducedMotion) { this.startTimer(); }\r\n
            this.$watch('paused', (val) => {\r\n
                if (val) { clearInterval(this.timer); this.timer = null; }\r\n
                else if (!this.reducedMotion) { this.startTimer(); }\r\n
            });\r\n
            window.addEventListener('resize', () => { this.windowWidth = window.innerWidth; });\r\n
        },\r\n
        destroy() { clearInterval(this.timer); },\r\n
        startTimer() { this.timer = setInterval(() => { this.slide = (this.slide + 1) % this.totalSlides; }, 5000); },\r\n
        prev() { this.slide = (this.slide - 1 + this.totalSlides) % this.totalSlides; this.resetTimer(); },\r\n
        next() { this.slide = (this.slide + 1) % this.totalSlides; this.resetTimer(); },\r\n
        resetTimer() { clearInterval(this.timer); this.timer = null; if (!this.reducedMotion) { this.startTimer(); } },\r\n
        goTo(i) { this.slide = i; this.resetTimer(); },\r\n
        getSlidePercent() {\r\n
            const visible = this.windowWidth < 640 ? 1 : this.windowWidth < 768 ? 2 : 3;\r\n
            const effectiveSlides = Math.min(visible, this.totalSlides);\r\n
            return this.totalSlides > 0 ? 100 / effectiveSlides : 100;\r\n
        },\r\n
        getCardStyle() {\r\n
            const visible = this.windowWidth < 640 ? 1 : this.windowWidth < 768 ? 2 : 3;\r\n
            const effectiveSlides = Math.min(visible, this.totalSlides);\r\n
            const gap = 16;\r\n
            const totalGap = (effectiveSlides - 1) * gap;\r\n
            return `width: calc((100% - ${totalGap}px) / ${effectiveSlides})`;\r\n
        },\r\n
    }">\r\n
        <div class="mb-6 md:mb-8">\r\n
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">\r\n
                <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-primary-50 dark:bg-primary-500/10 text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400 text-[11px] font-semibold px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-primary-100 dark:border-primary-500/20">\r\n
                    <i class="ri-megaphone-2-fill text-[10px]"></i> Promo Spesial\r\n
                </span>\r\n
                <span class="h-px flex-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-200 dark:from-slate-700 to-transparent"></span>\r\n
            </div>\r\n
            <h2 class="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-slate-50 tracking-tight">Penawaran Terbaik untuk Pencari Kos</h2>\r\n
            <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-slate-400">Temukan promo menarik dari partner kami</p>\r\n
        </div>\r\n
\r\n
        <div class="relative">\r\n
            <div class="overflow-hidden" @mouseenter="paused = true" @mouseleave="paused = false" @focusin="paused = true" @focusout="paused = false">\r\n
                <div class="flex gap-4 transition-transform duration-500 ease-out" :style="'transform: translateX(-' + slide * getSlidePercent() + '%)'">\r\n
                                                                    <div class="shrink-0" :style="getCardStyle()">\r\n
                            <a href="http://127.0.0.1:8000/ad/click/1?placement=homepage" data-ad-id="1" class="group relative flex flex-col h-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-[#fff3ee] via-white to-[#ffe2d5] dark:from-[#27120e] dark:via-[#3a1c13] dark:to-[#27120e] border border-[#ee4d2d]/25 hover:border-[#ee4d2d]/45 dark:border-[#ff7a5c]/25 dark:hover:border-[#ff7a5c]/45 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-300">\r\n
                                <div class="relative aspect-[16/9] overflow-hidden bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 shrink-0">\r\n
                                                                            <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-[#ee4d2d]/10 text-[#ee4d2d] dark:bg-[#ff7a5c]/10 dark:text-[#ff9a7c] flex items-center justify-center p-6">\r\n
                                                                                            <img src="/images/partners/shopee.svg" alt="Shopee" class="max-h-10 max-w-[120px] object-contain bg-white rounded-xl p-3 shadow-sm" onerror="if(!this.dataset.fallbackDone){this.dataset.fallbackDone='1';this.style.display='none';var f=this.nextElementSibling;if(f) f.style.display='flex';}"><span style="display:none" class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-[#cc2e14] to-[#d33a1d] text-white shadow-lg flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold uppercase">S</span>\r\n

    \n
    <script>\n
        (function () {\n
            var STORAGE_KEY = 'kosmanager:recently_viewed';\n
            var MAX_ITEMS = 6;\n
\n
            window.recentlyViewed = function () {\n
                return {\n
                    recent: [],\n
                    loading: true,\n
                    init() {\n
                        try {\n
                            var raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');\n
                            this.recent = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw.filter(function (i) { return i && i.url; }).slice(0, MAX_ITEMS) : [];\n
                        } catch (e) {\n
                            this.recent = [];\n
                        }\n
                        this.loading = false;\n
                    },\n
                    clearRecent() {\n
                        try { localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY); } catch (e) {}\n
                        this.recent = [];\n
                    }\n
                };\n
            };\n
\n
            // Record the viewed kos on the detail page\n
            var record = document.getElementById('record-view');\n
            if (record) {\n
                try {\n
                    var item = JSON.parse(record.textContent);\n
                    var list = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');\n
                    list = Array.isArray(list) ? list.filter(function (i) { return i.id !== item.id; }) : [];\n
                    list.unshift(item);\n
                    list = list.slice(0, MAX_ITEMS);\n
                    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(list));\n
                } catch (e) {}\n
            }\n
        })();\n
    </script>\n
            </main>\n
        </div>\n
    </div>\n

<section aria-label="Promo Partner" class="relative bg-gradient-to-b from-gray-50/80 to-white dark:from-slate-700/40 dark:to-surface-dark py-8 md:py-10">\r\n
    <div class="relative max-w-[1170px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6" x-data="{\r\n
        slide: 0,\r\n
        timer: null,\r\n
        totalSlides: 3,\r\n
        paused: false,\r\n
        windowWidth: window.innerWidth,\r\n
        reducedMotion: window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches,\r\n
        init() {\r\n
            if (!this.reducedMotion) { this.startTimer(); }\r\n
            this.$watch('paused', (val) => {\r\n
                if (val) { clearInterval(this.timer); this.timer = null; }\r\n
                else if (!this.reducedMotion) { this.startTimer(); }\r\n
            });\r\n
            window.addEventListener('resize', () => { this.windowWidth = window.innerWidth; });\r\n
        },\r\n
        destroy() { clearInterval(this.timer); },\r\n
        startTimer() { this.timer = setInterval(() => { this.slide = (this.slide + 1) % this.totalSlides; }, 5000); },\r\n
        prev() { this.slide = (this.slide - 1 + this.totalSlides) % this.totalSlides; this.resetTimer(); },\r\n
        next() { this.slide = (this.slide + 1) % this.totalSlides; this.resetTimer(); },\r\n
        resetTimer() { clearInterval(this.timer); this.timer = null; if (!this.reducedMotion) { this.startTimer(); } },\r\n
        goTo(i) { this.slide = i; this.resetTimer(); },\r\n
        getSlidePercent() {\r\n
            const visible = this.windowWidth < 640 ? 1 : this.windowWidth < 768 ? 2 : 3;\r\n
            const effectiveSlides = Math.min(visible, this.totalSlides);\r\n
            return this.totalSlides > 0 ? 100 / effectiveSlides : 100;\r\n
        },\r\n
        getCardStyle() {\r\n
            const visible = this.windowWidth < 640 ? 1 : this.windowWidth < 768 ? 2 : 3;\r\n
            const effectiveSlides = Math.min(visible, this.totalSlides);\r\n
            const gap = 16;\r\n
            const totalGap = (effectiveSlides - 1) * gap;\r\n
            return `width: calc((100% - ${totalGap}px) / ${effectiveSlides})`;\r\n
        },\r\n
    }">\r\n
        <div class="mb-6 md:mb-8">\r\n
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">\r\n
                <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-primary-50 dark:bg-primary-500/10 text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400 text-[11px] font-semibold px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-primary-100 dark:border-primary-500/20">\r\n
                    <i class="ri-megaphone-2-fill text-[10px]"></i> Promo Spesial\r\n

                <span class="h-px flex-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-200 dark:from-slate-700 to-transparent"></span>\r\n

                                                 <label for="phone" class="field-label"><i class="ri-whatsapp-line text-emerald-500 mr-1"></i> WhatsApp <span class="text-red-500">*</span></label>\n
                                                 <input type="text" id="phone" name="phone" value="" required inputmode="tel" maxlength="20" placeholder="0812xxxxxxx"\n
                                                        class="w-full rounded-xl border-slate-200 dark:border-white/[0.08] dark:bg-surface-elevated dark:text-slate-50 text-sm px-4 py-3 focus:border-primary-500 focus:ring-primary-500 shadow-sm">\n
                                                 <p class="mt-1.5 text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400"><i class="ri-information-line mr-1"></i> Bisa +62/62 atau 08 — otomatis dinormalisasi.</p>\n
                                                                                              </div>\n

                                                           class="w-full rounded-xl border-slate-200 dark:border-white/[0.08] dark:bg-surface-elevated dark:text-slate-50 text-sm px-4 py-3 focus:border-primary-500 focus:ring-primary-500 shadow-sm"></textarea>\n

                                                   data-default-end="2026-12-14">\n

                                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2.5"><span class="grid w-6 h-6 place-items-center rounded-full bg-slate-900 dark:bg-white text-white dark:text-slate-900 shrink-0"><i class="ri-customer-service-2-line text-xs"></i></span> Check-in diproses pengelola saat kedatangan</li>\n

                                            <button type="submit" :disabled="submitting"\n

                                                <template x-if="!submitting">\n
                                                    <span class="flex items-center gap-2"><i class="ri-calendar-check-line"></i> Konfirmasi Booking</span>\n
                                                </template>\n
                                                <template x-if="submitting" x-cloak>\n
                                                    <span class="flex items-center gap-2"><i class="ri-loader-4-line animate-spin"></i> Memproses...</span>\n
                                                </template>\n

             <script>\n
            (function () {\n
                var fmt = function (n) { return 'Rp ' + Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID'); };\n
                var roomSel = document.getElementById('kamar-select');\n
                var startIn = document.getElementById('start-date');\n
                var endIn = document.getElementById('end-date');\n
                if (!roomSel) return;\n
\n
                var occupantInput = document.getElementById('occupant-count');\n
                var occupantMinus = document.getElementById('occupant-minus');\n
                var occupantPlus = document.getElementById('occupant-plus');\n
                var occupantCapacity = document.getElementById('occupant-capacity');\n
                var occupantInviteHelp = document.getElementById('occupant-invite-help');\n
                var elKamar = document.getElementById('sum-kamar');\n
                var elOccupants = document.getElementById('sum-occupants');\n
                var elBilling = document.getElementById('sum-billing');\n
                var elType = document.getElementById('sum-type');\n
                var elPeriode = document.getElementById('sum-periode');\n
                var elTotal = document.getElementById('sum-total');\n
                var elOccupantHelp = document.getElementById('sum-occupant-help');\n
                var elTotalNote = document.getElementById('sum-total-note');\n
                var roomPhotoWrap = document.getElementById('room-photo-wrap');\n
                var roomPhotoImg = document.getElementById('room-photo-img');\n
                var roomPhotoLetter = document.getElementById('room-photo-letter');\n
                var billingFieldset = document.getElementById('billing-mode-fieldset');\n
                var billingHelp = document.getElementById('billing-mode-help');\n
\n
                function daysBetween(a, b) {\n
                    var d1 = new Date(a + 'T00:00:00');\n
                    var d2 = new Date(b + 'T00:00:00');\n
                    return Math.round((d2 - d1) / 86400000);\n
                }\n
                function clampOccupant() {\n
                    var v = parseInt(occupantInput.value, 10) || 1;\n
                    var cap = parseInt((roomSel.options[roomSel.selectedIndex] || {}).dataset.capacity || '10', 10) || 10;\n
                    if (v < 1) v = 1; if (v > cap) v = cap; if (v > 10) v = 10;\n
                    occupantInput.value = v; return v;\n
                }\n
                function billingLabel(val) { return val === 'shared' ? 'Patungan' : 'Tanggung sendiri'; }\n
                function updateOccupantUI() {\n
                    var opt = roomSel.options[roomSel.selectedIndex];\n
                    var cap = opt && opt.value ? parseInt(opt.dataset.capacity || '1', 10) || 1 : 1;\n
                    var n = clampOccupant();\n
                    if (!opt || !opt.value) { occupantCapacity.textContent = ''; occupantInviteHelp.classList.add('hidden'); occupantInput.max = 10; return; }\n
                    occupantInput.max = Math.min(cap, 10);\n
                    occupantCapacity.textContent = cap > 1 ? 'Kamar ini untuk hingga ' + cap + ' orang.' : 'Kamar ini untuk 1 orang.';\n
                    if (n > 1 && cap > 1) occupantInviteHelp.classList.remove('hidden'); else occupantInviteHelp.classList.add('hidden');\n
                }\n
\n
                function updateBillingMode() {\n
                    if (!billingFieldset) return;\n
                    var opt = roomSel.options[roomSel.selectedIndex];\n
                    if (!opt || !opt.value) {\n
                        billingFieldset.classList.add('hidden');\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
                    var bm = (opt.dataset.billingMode || 'personal').toLowerCase();\n
                    if (bm === 'shared') {\n
                        billingFieldset.classList.remove('hidden');\n
                        if (billingHelp) {\n
                            billingHelp.textContent = 'Kos ini mendukung patungan. Pilih Patungan jika biaya ingin dibagi penghuni.';\n
                            billingHelp.classList.remove('hidden');\n
                        }\n
                    } else {\n
                        billingFieldset.classList.add('hidden');\n
                        var personal = billingFieldset.querySelector('input[value="personal"]');\n
                        if (personal) personal.checked = true;\n
                        if (billingHelp) billingHelp.classList.add('hidden');\n
                    }\n
                }\n
\n
                function update() {\n
                    var opt = roomSel.options[roomSel.selectedIndex];\n
                    if (!opt || !opt.value) {\n
                        elKamar.textContent = '—';\n
                        if (elOccupants) elOccupants.textContent = '—';\n
                        if (elBilling) elBilling.textContent = '—';\n
                        elType.textContent = '—';\n
                        elPeriode.textContent = '—';\n
                        elTotal.textContent = '—';\n
                        if (elOccupantHelp) elOccupantHelp.classList.add('hidden');\n
                        if (elTotalNote) elTotalNote.classList.add('hidden');\n
                        roomPhotoWrap.classList.add('hidden');\n
                        updateBillingMode(); updateOccupantUI(); return;\n
                    }\n
                    var rentalType = document.querySelector('input[name="rental_type"]:checked').value;\n
                    var price = Number(opt.dataset[rentalType] || 0);\n
                    var unitLabels = { daily: '/hari', weekly: '/mgg', monthly: '/bulan' };\n
                    var typeLabels = { daily: 'Harian', weekly: 'Mingguan', monthly: 'Bulanan' };\n
                    var unit = unitLabels[rentalType] || '/bulan';\n
\n
                    var n = clampOccupant();\n
                    var billingVal = (document.querySelector('input[name="billing_mode"]:checked') || {}).value || 'personal';\n
                    var cap = parseInt(opt.dataset.capacity || '1', 10) || 1;\n
                    elKamar.textContent = opt.dataset.room || ('Kamar #' + opt.value);\n
                    if (elOccupants) elOccupants.textContent = n + ' orang' + (n === 1 ? '' : ' · ' + cap + ' maks');\n
                    if (elBilling) elBilling.textContent = billingLabel(billingVal);\n
                    elType.textContent = (typeLabels[rentalType] || 'Bulanan') + ' · ' + fmt(price) + unit;\n
\n
                    var s = startIn.value, e = endIn.value;\n
                    var total = 0;\n
                    if (s && e && s <= e) {\n
                        var inclusiveDays = daysBetween(s, e) + 1;\n
                        elPeriode.textContent = s + ' s/d ' + e + ' (' + inclusiveDays + ' hari)';\n
                        if (rentalType === 'daily') total = price * inclusiveDays;\n
                        else if (rentalType === 'weekly') total = price * Math.ceil(inclusiveDays / 7);\n
                        else { var sd = new Date(s + 'T00:00:00'), ed = new Date(e + 'T00:00:00'); total = price * ( (ed.getFullYear() - sd.getFullYear()) * 12 + (ed.getMonth() - sd.getMonth()) + 1 ); }\n
                    } else elPeriode.textContent = 'Tanggal tidak valid';\n
                    elTotal.textContent = total > 0 ? fmt(total) : (price > 0 ? fmt(price) : 'Hubungi pemilik');\n
                    if (elOccupantHelp) {\n
                        if (n > 1) { elOccupantHelp.textContent = 'Kamu bisa mengundang teman setelah booking jadi.'; elOccupantHelp.classList.remove('hidden'); }\n
                        else { elOccupantHelp.textContent = 'Booking untuk 1 orang.'; elOccupantHelp.classList.remove('hidden'); }\n
                    }\n
                    if (elTotalNote) {\n
                        if (billingVal === 'shared' && n > 1) { elTotalNote.textContent = 'Total akan dibagi sesuai jumlah penghuni.'; elTotalNote.classList.remove('hidden'); }\n
                        else if (billingVal === 'shared') { elTotalNote.textContent = 'Siap dibagi jika undang penghuni lain.'; elTotalNote.classList.remove('hidden'); }\n
                        else { elTotalNote.textContent = 'Total periode ini — dibayar sesuai tagihan.'; elTotalNote.classList.remove('hidden'); }\n
                    }\n
\n
                    var photo = opt.dataset.photo;\n
                    var roomLabel = (opt.dataset.room || '').split(' — ')[0] || '';\n
                    if (photo) {\n
                        roomPhotoImg.src = '/storage/' + photo;\n
                        roomPhotoImg.alt = 'Foto Kamar ' + roomLabel;\n
                        roomPhotoImg.style.display = '';\n
                        roomPhotoLetter.textContent = '';\n
                        roomPhotoWrap.classList.remove('hidden');\n
                    } else {\n
                        roomPhotoImg.src = '';\n
                        roomPhotoImg.style.display = 'none';\n
                        roomPhotoLetter.textContent = roomLabel.substring(0, 2);\n
                        roomPhotoWrap.classList.remove('hidden');\n
                    }\n
                    updateBillingMode(); updateOccupantUI();\n
                }\n
\n
                if (occupantMinus) occupantMinus.addEventListener('click', function(){ var v=Math.max(1,(parseInt(occupantInput.value,10)||1)-1); occupantInput.value=v; updateOccupantUI(); update(); });\n
                if (occupantPlus) occupantPlus.addEventListener('click', function(){ var cap=parseInt((roomSel.options[roomSel.selectedIndex]||{}).dataset.capacity||'10',10)||10; var v=Math.min(cap,(parseInt(occupantInput.value,10)||1)+1); occupantInput.value=v; updateOccupantUI(); update(); });\n
                if (occupantInput) { occupantInput.addEventListener('change', function(){ updateOccupantUI(); update(); }); occupantInput.addEventListener('input', function(){ updateOccupantUI(); update(); }); }\n
                [roomSel, startIn, endIn].forEach(function (el) { el.addEventListener('change', update); el.addEventListener('input', update); });\n
\n
                // Default tanggal mengikuti tipe sewa.\n
                // Saat tenant mengubah Tanggal Mulai → Tanggal Selesai otomatis mengikuti\n
                // (end > start; min end = start). Berlaku untuk SEMUA kos.\n
                function pad(n){ return (n<10?'0':'')+n; }\n
                function toYmd(d){ return d.getFullYear()+'-'+pad(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+pad(d.getDate()); }\n
                function addDaysYmd(ymd, days){ var d=new Date(ymd+'T00:00:00'); d.setDate(d.getDate()+days); return toYmd(d); }\n
                function addMonthNoOverflowYmd(ymd){ var d=new Date(ymd+'T00:00:00'); var day=d.getDate(); d.setDate(1); d.setMonth(d.getMonth()+1); var last=new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth()+1, 0).getDate(); d.setDate(Math.min(day, last)); return toYmd(d); }\n
                function defaultStartFor(type){\n
                    var t=new Date(); t.setHours(0,0,0,0);\n
                    if(type==='weekly'){ t.setDate(t.getDate()+7); }\n
                    else if(type==='daily'){ /* hari ini */ }\n
                    else { var day=t.getDate(); t.setDate(1); t.setMonth(t.getMonth()+1); var last=new Date(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth()+1, 0).getDate(); t.setDate(Math.min(day, last)); }\n
                    return toYmd(t);\n
                }\n
                // End selalu > start (daily +1, weekly +7, monthly +1 bulan)\n
                function defaultEndFor(startYmd, type){\n
                    if(!startYmd) startYmd = toYmd(new Date());\n
                    if(type==='weekly') return addDaysYmd(startYmd, 7);\n
                    if(type==='daily') return addDaysYmd(startYmd, 1);\n
                    return addMonthNoOverflowYmd(startYmd);\n
                }\n
                function currentRentalType(){ return (document.querySelector('input[name="rental_type"]:checked')||{}).value || 'monthly'; }\n
                function syncEndMinFromStart(){\n
                    if(startIn.value){ endIn.setAttribute('min', startIn.value); }\n
                }\n
                // Saat start berubah → end otomatis mengikuti periode tipe sewa\n
                function applyEndFollowingStart(){\n
                    var startVal = startIn.value || toYmd(new Date());\n
                    var type = currentRentalType();\n
                    var nextEnd = defaultEndFor(startVal, type);\n
                    endIn.value = nextEnd;\n
                    endIn.setAttribute('data-default-end', nextEnd);\n
                    endIn.setAttribute('min', startVal);\n
                    // Jika end masih <= start (edge), paksa minimal start+1 via periode di atas\n
                    if(endIn.value <= startVal && type !== 'daily'){\n
                        endIn.value = type==='weekly' ? addDaysYmd(startVal, 7) : addMonthNoOverflowYmd(startVal);\n
                    }\n
                }\n
                function applyDefaultsIfUntouched(type){\n
                    var prevStart = startIn.getAttribute('data-default-start') || '';\n
                    var prevEnd = endIn.getAttribute('data-default-end') || '';\n
                    var nextStart = defaultStartFor(type);\n
                    var nextEnd = defaultEndFor(nextStart, type);\n
                    if (!startIn.value || startIn.value === prevStart) {\n
                        startIn.value = nextStart;\n
                    }\n
                    if (!endIn.value || endIn.value === prevEnd || endIn.value <= (startIn.value||nextStart)) {\n
